  1. General Information

   1.1 Introduction

  The Bibi Hakimeh Oil field is located in Dezful Embayment area near the Persian Gulf
   at the eastern border of Dezful Embayment and the foothills of the Zagros Mountain
   range. The field lies 230km southeast of Ahwaz city and 50km north of Genaveh
   seaport.  It was discovered in 1961, and  it has produced oil since 1963. The main
   reservoirs of this field are Asmari and Bangestan reservoirs. Bibi Hakimeh structure is
  an asymmetric anticline with steeper southern flank which is surrounded by Rag-e Safid
  (NW), Sulabdar (SE), Garangan (N), Kilur Karim and Siah Makan (S) fields. This
   structure is an asymmetric anticline elongated NW-SE with steeper southern flank.

   BiBi Hakimeh EPD/EPC Project consist of Drilling 6 New Asmari/Bangestan wells and
  10 Re-entry Workover Wells.

                       Figure 1: Location of BiBi Hakimeh Oil Field in the Map

                Figure 2: BiBi Hakimeh Oil field – UGC Map of Structure (On top of Asmari Fm)

   1.3 Well Structure

   Well BH-144 is located on North west of crestal position of BiBi Hakimeh Structure, on
   the underground contour map at the top of Asmari formation.

  Due to producing excessing gas, production operation has been stopped by NISOC.
   the present program for this location includes opening a window in Base parts of
   Gachsaran formation Member-3 at 1840 m through 9 5/8” Casing (53.5#, N80, BTS)
   and  drill directional Re-Entry hole sections to Asmari reservoir and subsequently
   produce oil from this reservoir as Open Hole Completion by 7'' * 4 1/2" *-5K Standard
  Gas lift Completion String.

                      Figure 3 Well BH-144 Location with Respect to Old Near-By Wells

       1.7 Pore and Fracture Pressure Prediction

       As pore pressures and fracture pressures of non-reservoir formations of BiBi Hakimeh  oil field are not received from Client,
        approximate equivalent pore pressure of non-reservoir formations was predicted using Mud Weight history of Original well in each
         formation with regard to the Loss/Gain and stability situation of the well.

   1.8 Workover Program General Outline

     It is planned to Re-drill and complete a Deviated Producer well to produce oil from
   Asmari Reservoir (Zone B) of BiBi Hakimeh Oil Filed at well BH-144 (W040S location)
   as Second workover program of the well (W.O.2, S.T.2).

   BH-144 will be Re-drilled by opening a window at 1840 m TVD (KOP @ 9 5/8” Casing,
   Gs#3) and proceeding directionally to 2449.4 m MD (Well TD) according to directional
    drilling program, to Produce oil from Asmari Formation Zone B. 7” liner will cover
   Gachsaran Formation to Cap rock, and 5” liner will be used as gas exclusion liner to
   cover gas bearing zones of Asmari formation the well will be plugged back from TD to
   5” liner shoe depth and after running and cementing 5” liner, Magneset plug will be
    drilled out to Final TD of the well. Exact 5” liner shoe depth will be determined according
    to log evaluation result. the well will be completed in Asmari formation as Directional
  Open hole Completion by Using 7'' * 4 ½” -5K Standard Gas lift Completion String.

   General Outlines of BH-144 W.O.2, S.T.2 Program Includes:

    1- Rig Up Workover Rig, Secure and Kill the well

    2- POOH 1603 m, 2 7/8” Kick-Off Completion String

    3- Well Clean Out Trip Inside Existing 9 5/8” Casing and 5” liner.

    4- Well Cement Plug Back (at 2200 m)

    5- Changing Existing Tubing Head Spool to New One

    6- Window Opening at 1840 m (KOP @ 9 5/8” CSG, Gs.Mbr.-3), Side Tracking

    7- 8 1/2” Hole Section Drilling (1840-2120m)

    8- 7” Liner Running (1720-2120 m), Cementing and L.Lap testing

    9- 7” Liner Cement Quality logging (SBT-VDL-CCL-GR)

    10- Clean Out Inside 7” Liner, Shoe Drill out

    11- 6 1/8” Hole Section Drilling (2120-2449.4 m)

    12- Open hole Reservoir Evaluation Logging

  2. Geological Information

   2.1 Formation Description

    Stratigraphic succession of well BH-144 W.O.2 S.T.2 expected to be drilled has been
   presented from K.O.P. @ 1840 m inside of Gachsaran Fm. Mbr.-3 to Asmari Fm. (total
   depth @ 2449.4m), a brief stratigraphic succession in Bibi Hakimeh field has been
   described as follows:

❖ Gachsaran Fm. F/ 1840 (K.O.P) T/ 2176.73 m:

   Gachsaran Formation generally consists of a repetition of anhydrite, grey and red marl
   as well as salt and some limestone beds. This formation mainly is divided into seven
    different members. In this well, Window would be opened in Gachsaran members would
   be drilled in this well new Re-Entry Section:

        Member-3 F/1751 T/1888.55 m:

          This member mainly consists of interbeds of white anhydrite, soft grey marl and
             slightly limestone.

        Member-2 F/1888.55 T/ 2114.61 m:
          This member mainly consists of salt with some anhydrite and grey marl.

        Member-1 (Cap Rock) F/ 2114.61 m T/ 2176.3 m: Top of Cap Rock is mainly
          recognized by the anhydrite bed KB/A, existing anhydrites are characterized by
            radial and Carb crystals (KB/A), black bituminous shale is the KB/B then the
           layers of grey marl, anhydrite and limestone, which include the Key Beds C, D,
       E and F are placed. Cap Rock has been divided into six key beds as follows:

           Key Bed A: White & coarse crystalline anhydrite with Carb "D" and quartz

           Key Bed B: Dark reddish brown, bituminous shale and marl

           Key Bed C: Cream-brownish, fine crystalline, pseudo oolitic limestone

           Key Bed D: Light brown-cream, medium hard, fine to medium crystalline,
                    silty, sandy dolomitic limestone.

           Key Bed E: Light brown, silty, limestone, barren

           Key Bed F: Cream-light brown, marly, anhydritic limestone key beds

   For BH-144, It has planned to Open the 8 1/2” window at depth of 1840 m (KOP), Within
   Base of Gachsaran Formation and Continue directionally to Cap Rock. 7” liner would
   be set at 2120 m inside Caprock.

❖ Asmari Fm. F/ 2176.73 m to 2449.4m (TD)

   This  formation  mainly  consists  of  dolomitic  limestone and  limestone  (bioclast

                                                                                  Page 31 of 151

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                   Workover Program For
                     Well: BH-144, W.O.2, S.T.2
                      BiBi Hakimeh Oil Field

Page 32 of 151                 Doc. No: BH-15-SM-110-DR-DM-1789-D02                   Rev:02

   wackstone- packstone) with thin layers of anhydrite and greyish green shale and marl.
   The top of Asmari Formation consists of white-off white cream to light grey type I, II
   dolomitic argillaceous limestone. The middle part of this formation consists of light
   brown-buff, white, chalky limestone with interbedded thin layers of greyish-green shale
   and marls. In the lower part are grey, cream, some argillaceous, hard, fossiliferous
   limestone. Based on the micropaleontological data, Asmari Formation can be divided
    into the Upper, Middle and Lower Asmari intervals which are discussed below:
   (paleontological report of BH-120).

        Upper Asmari: This interval consists of dolomitic limestone and limestone
            (bioclast wackestone-packstone) and  is marked by presence  of  benthic
           microfossils such as: Discorbis sp., Rotalia sp., Peneroplis spp., Elphidium sp.,
           Dendritina rangi, Asterigerina rotula, Amphistegina sp., Tubucellaria sp. and
             Miliolids.

         Middle Asmari: This part mainly consists of dolomitic limestone, limestone
          (wackestone- packstone) and dolomite in some parts.

          This part of the Asmari Formation contains diagnostic microfossils such as:
            Austrotrillina  howchini,  Operculina  sp.,  Discorbis  sp.,  Tubucellaria  sp.,
          Peneroplis evolutus, Dendritina rangi, Elphidium sp., Archaias sp., Rotalia
            viennotti, Meandropsina anahensi., Asterigerina rotula, Miliolids and Valvulinids

        Lower  Asmari:   It  mainly  consists  of  limestone  (bioclast  wackestone-
          packstone).

        The Lower Asmari is characterized by presence of the following microfossils:
         Nummulites fichteli/intermedius, Rotalia sp., Tubucellaria sp., Rotalia viennotti,
          Operculina complanata, Lepidocyclina sp., Elphidium sp., Asterigerina rotula,
         Nummulites sp., Nummulites vascus, Austrotrillina paucialveolata.

   and mud logging crew shall take sample based on well site geologist instructions. In
   deviated interval well site geologist should take care of sampling and collecting sample

  3. BH-144 Drilling and Workover History

   198 Offset wells have been drilled in BiBi Hakimeh Oil Field, previously. Drilling of the
    First Well (BH-001) was started at 1339/10/28 and Rig released at 1340/05/25.

   For investigating the potential drilling problems of Re-Entry Hole Sections of BH-144,
   the best reference is Main Well Drilling and Workover History. Well BH-144 Drilling,
   Workover #1 and Production Histories are presented as Below.

   3.1 Initial Drilling (Vertical Well)

   Highlights:

   This well is located on west south flank of BiBi Hakimeh structure between well Nos. 86
  & 6. Drilling operation was continued F/Sur. In Mishan Fm. Down to 2375 m. Total Depth
    in Asmari Fm. A normal stratigraphical sequence was drilled down (except fault action
    in Gs. Mbr.4) to total depth & top of Asmari Fm. Was picked up @2202 m. (-1649mc.),
   85 m. below forecast depth DST No.1 was carried out over first 50 m of Asmari Fm. At
   reverse out it was recovered gas.

   The evaluation logs were run & the well was completed as an open hole Asmari oil
   producer. Rig released on: 1386/09/06.

   26" Hole Section:

    Drilling was started on 1386/07/5 from surface (Mishan Fm.) to 61m, set and cemented
   18 5/8” casing shoe at 59 m and 22 bbl cement return.

   17 1/2" Hole Section:

   This hole was started from 61 m to 186 m with 66 pcf bentonite mud, and 80 bph mud
    loss. Pump Mica Pill to cure the loss and continued drilling with 8-30 bph loss to 502 m.
   observed complete loss at 520 m and set cement plug at this depth. Drilling was
   resumed to 623 m and set and cemented 13 3/8” casing shoe at 622 m with 150 bbl
   cement return.

   12 1/4" Hole Section:

   Before starting the new section, pumped 45 bbl cement slurry in 18 5/8” * 13 3/8” casing
   annulus by trying to inject with 300 psi pumping pressure without success.

   This hole was started with 105 pcf mud weight, due to increase the calcium in mud
    return, increased mud weight to 108 pcf at 924 m. continued drilling from 1107 m to
   1332 m with 120-127 pcf mud weight, according to nearby well problem mud weight
   was increased to 129 pcf at 1379 m with 60-80 bph loss. Trip out the drilling string to
   change the Bit and decreased mud weight to 125 pcf, drilling was resumed to 2003.5 m
   and perform CDR/GR log at this depth. Continue drilling to 2145 m and set and
   cemented 9 5/8 “casing shoe at 2145 m with 200 bbl cement return.

   8 1/2" Hole Section:

   This hole was started with 55.5 pcf emulsion mud and observed complete loss at 2208
   m, pumped shell-fish pill to cure the mud loss and successful. DST test was performed
   over first 50 m of Asmari formation at reverse out  it was recovered gas. Continued
    drilling to 2375 m with 30-40 bph, set magneset plug from 2375 m to 2303 m. Run and
   cemented 7” liner from 2021 to 2303 m. tag top of cement at 2004m. Perform 7” L.Lap
   dry test with 856 m water caution with good results.  drilled out LDC, Cement and
   magneset to total depth 2375 m and displace the well by diesel.
   2 7/8” Tubing was run to 1602.13 m and the well was secured and released the Rig on
   1386/09/06.

   3.2 First Workover (W.O.1, S.T.1)

   This well was Asmari oil producer but due to obstruction in 7” Liner & Unsuccessful to
   pass on, the well sidetracked from 2214 m in Asmari Formation. Continued directional
    drilling to 2453 m. (Total depth), Reservoir evaluation logs were run & set 5” Liner. The
    well was completed as an open hole Asmari oil producer & Rig released on 1388/06/05.

   Reason of Workover #1:
    Oil Producer Well, shut-in due to excessive gas production.

   Workover#1, S.T.1 History:
        1. Work-Over started at 1388/04/16. Wellhead pressure was 1400 psi and Kill line
        was Prepared and pumped 1100 bbl water to kill the well. After set 2 31/64” plug
           inside tubing hanger, X mass tree was nipple down. BOP was installed, POOH
         and L/D 2 7/8” kick off string, clean out the well with 8 ½” & 6 1/8” mill to 7” liner
           lap and to 2220 m. Scrap inside of 7” liner (whipstock setting depth). Mud loss
        was 60-80 bph. While displaced the well with 55.5 pcf oil-based mud.
        2. Run and set Whipstock at 2216 m.
        3. Commence Window opening from 2212 m to 2216.5 m, while start directional
             drilling from 2214 m (K.O.P) to 2453 m (TD) with M.B.U.A 47 deg and 132.8 deg
          (Azimuth) at 2346.5 m with 6-40 bph loss and pumped shell fish during drilling.
        4.  Perform TLC log and set magneset, then drill out to 2405 m.
        5.  Set and cement 5” liner from 1999 m MD to 2405 m MD.
        6.  Dry test was performed and not observed leakage at liner lap.
        7. Run 1600 m 2 7/8” tubing, secured the well and nipple down BOP.
        8. N/U wellhead and pressure test same, OK.
        9. R/U CTU, RIH to top of open hole, perform injectivity test, after pumping 500 bbl
         HCl acid, complete loss occurred, and observed the well in vacuum condition.
       Pump 4400-gallon liquid N2 in order to flow the well without any results, finally
           flow the well with several choke sizes to burning pit.
       10. The rig was released on 1388/06/05.

   3.3 Production History

   Well drilling was started on 1386/07/05. Sets and cemented 18 5/8 casing shoe at 59
  m MD and 13 3/8 casing shoe at 622 m MD. Drilling was resumed to 2145 m with 12
   1/4" Bit and 105-129 pcf mud then set and cemented 9 5/8” at 2145 m. continued drilling
   with 8 1/2" Bit and 55.5 pcf emulsion mud to 2375 m and observed complete loss at
   2208 m and 2215 m. ran full suite logs. 7” liner was set and cemented 2303 m and dry
    test was performed. DST test was performed of Asmari formation at reverse out it was
   recovered gas. Continue drilling with 6 1/8” Bit and 55.5 pcf mud to 2375 m. well was
   cleaned and displace by diesel. After obtaining cement bond logs (CBL/VDL) from 2021
    to 2303 m, 2 7/8" tubing string of 1602 m was run. installing the wellhead, injectivity test
   was conducted in the open hole, with a maximum injection rate of 5 bpm at 1000 psi.
   The well was secured, and the rig was released on 1386/09/06.

   Production History Before Workover #1:

        1.  After installing surface facilities, the well was flowed to the burn pit for cleaning
         and flow evaluation using N2 gas on 86/10/06. Due to pressure drop and
           unproductivity of the well, the well was closed on86/10/8.
        2.  Acidizing operations were conducted on 86/10/25 using 8,000 gallons of 28%
           hydrochloric acid via a coiled tubing unit. The injectivity rate at the end of the
          operation was 8 bpm at 1200 psi. The well was then flowed to the burn pit using
        N2 gas, with the final status as follows:

        3. To reduce, pressure drop and improve communication with the formation, an
          extensive acidizing operation was conducted on 86/11/25 using 16,000 gallons
           of 28% hydrochloric acid and 20,000 gallons of emulsified acid at a maximum
          pressure (1500 psi) and a maximum rate of 21 bpm. The well was then flowed
           to the burn pit using N2 gas for cleaning and due to excessive gas production
         and increased flow pressure the well was closed on 86/11/27
        4.  After stabilizing the flow, the well underwent a temperature survey on 86/12/6,
         which confirmed gas entry from the 7” liner shoe.

  Summary of workover#1:

        The well workover began on 88/04/16. whipstock was installed, and open the
         window inside 7" liner from 2212 to 2216.5 m by 6 1/8" bit. The sidetrack was
             drilled to final depth of 2453 m with a maximum inclination of 47.94 degrees
        (KOP = 2214 meters).
         The loss rates were as follows:

                                  Interval     Loss Rate

                         (mMD)        (BPH)

                           2220-2242         2-6

                           2242-2278        20

                           2299-2453       25-35

        AIT/BHC/GR/TLD/HGNS/HNGC logs were run from 2200 m to 2450 m. 5" liner
           (L-80, 18#, FL4S) was run and cemented from 1999 to 2405 m. packer was set
           at 1980 m, dry test and pressure test was conducted at the top of the 5" liner,
         showing no bubbles. Magneset and cement was drill out to 2453 m with 55.5 pcf
          emulsion mud. Perform CBL/VDL/CCL logs from 1999 m to 2405 m.
         166 joints of 2 7/8" tubing (L-80, 6.5# EUE) with accessories and NO-GO 2.313”
         were run to the well, total length :1600 m. The blowout preventer was removed,
         and the standard wellhead was installed and pressure tested. The open hole
        was stimulated in two stages using 4,000 gallons of 28% hydrochloric acid,
         observed complete loss, the well was into the suction condition.
           After acidizing, the well was flowed using 7,000 gallons of nitrogen through 1
           1/2" and then a 1 1/4" choke, with diesel reported as the return fluid. The well
        was secured, and the rig was released on 88/06/05.

 3.5 Drilling Challenges

Gachsaran Formation:

  ❖ While drilling in massive salt formation of Gachsaran Formation, be careful of well trajectory
        control and the salt’s cave-in/dissolving. In this well Gachsaran formation has shown to be a
        thin layer with missing members.
  ❖ High pressure Zones of Gachsaran are potential zones for well flowing. Close monitoring of
        well condition in Gachsaran drilling is a must.
  ❖ Formation loss is expected to happen in base of Gachsaran Formation as observed in offset
       wells of the field. If it Is probable to confront with severe losses, L/D Stabilizer before drilling
       through loss zones.
  ❖  Maintain enough overbalance while drilling through Gachsaran Formation.
  ❖ Care should be given to detect the cap rock precisely and not to over drill the cap rock.

Asmari Formation:

  ❖ Formation Fluid from Asmari formation containing harmful hydrogen sulfide (H2S) has the
        risks to harm site personnel during the operation.
  ❖  In this Well, Asmari formation is occupied by Reservoir Gas /oil/ water. According to the
       information provided by the company, H2S content of Asmari Formation oil is 1800 ppm. All
        site personnel should pay more attention on H2S hazards, and all H2S equipment should be
        in good condition.
  ❖ There is a High risk of Mud losses followed by Gas flow in top of Asmari formation. Care
       should be given to close monitoring of well Condition, Keeping the well full all the time, regular
       function and pressure testing of BOP equipment, Regular H2S and Well Control Drills are
        obligatory. Rotating head should be used to control any potential gas flow. Gas Cap Drilling
       Policy should be followed while drilling Asmari formation.
  ❖  In case of facing with severe complete losses, due to risk of fast gas migration to surface,
        well should be closed immediately to take the well under control.
  ❖ Asmari Formation is prone to get the string stuck in case of well instability due to severe
       losses.
  ❖ Special Care Is Needed for BiBi Hakimeh Oil Field:
  ❖  BiBi Hakimeh Oil field drilling operation comprises of different drilling challenges most of
      which may happen simultaneously such as complete loss in gas cap followed by gas flow,
      complete loss in fractured zone causing the string to get stuck, wellbore instability due to
        highly depleted - fractured permeable formations with partial to complete losses of drilling
         fluid.

  ❖   it is very important to consider well control policies strictly, meanwhile drilling operation and
       other operations.  In case of Operation taking long time more than predicted timing, BOP
       Tests and well Control policies should be implemented according to NISOC POLICY.

  4. Detailed Sequence of Operation

   For the sake of briefness and Clarity of the workover program, Detailed procedures and
   instructions for each operation of all sections, will be issued and sent to rig in due time
   before performing the operation.

   4.1 Rig Up, Secure and Kill the Well

        1. Move and rig up Workover rig over the well BH-144.
        2. Read and record the shut-in wellhead pressure and annuluses pressure.
        3. Check all Wellhead p-seals and all valves for any leakage. Check T.H.S Gate valves
          Performance.
        4. Hook up kill and Choke lines to annuals & tubing sides and pressure test to 3000 Psi .
        5. Record and Report wellhead and Annulus Shut-in Pressures. In case of existing Annulus
          pressure, bleed, wait and monitor for possible increase.

             Note:

                    -     if observed any pressure build up in casing annuluses before taking any action,
                Main Client Representative and drilling office should be informed.
                    -  check and report all wellhead valve functionality and integrity.
                    -  H2S Services should be rigged up before Start of Operation.
                    -  Shear Rams, Rotating Head, extra water tanks & all safety regulations must be
                 considered as NISOC policy before  start  of operation and with regard  to
                   operational concerns.

        6.  Pressure tests the lines and X-mas tree for any probable leakage. Bullhead the well
          through tubing & Annulus simultaneously by 62.4 PCF fresh water, to kill the well. Closely
          monitor the pressure while killing the well.

             Note:

                    -   Control pumping Pressure to avoid existing casings and liner damage. Refer to
                   section  9.2  for tubular characteristics of the well. Consider de-ration of old
                 casings when performing pressurized treatments.

        7.  Circulate cross well head & make sure the well is static. Observe the well to make sure of
           well static condition.

             Note:

              Kill Fluid Weight (PCF)  Overbalance Depth (m)  Overbalance Pressure (Psi)

                                    2408 m MD / 2367.5 m
                    62.4                 TVD                    1239
                               Top of open hole

                    -   62.4 PCF fresh water will impose 1239 psi over pressure to the Asmari reservoir
                    at top of open hole (2408 m MD / 2367.5 m TVD).

────────────────────────────────────────────
                    Workover Program For
                       Well: BH-144, W.O.2, S.T.2
                       BiBi Hakimeh Oil Field

Page 59 of 151                  Doc. No: BH-15-SM-110-DR-DM-1789-D02                        Rev:02

                    -   Note: It has been supposed that the well is completely occupied by reservoir gas
                   with pressure gradient of 0.052 Psi/ft and reference depth and pressure of -600
             mTVDss and 1920 Psi, respectively.
                    -   Weight up materials (salt & Limestone) should be ready on board for contingency
                use to kill the well.
                    -   Perform Well Control and H2S Drills prior to start of killing Operation.
                    -  Use LCM Pill to cure losses after Well killing.

   4.2 POOH 2 7/8” Kick-Off Completion String

        1. R/U Slickline, Open X-mas Tree Cap and Dipping Valve, install and test lubricator, drift
        Check w/proper size (min:2.313") gauge ring to top of No-Go landing nipple.

        2.  Run & Set plug & Prong in no go landing nipple (F-Baker 2.313”) with Slickline and
          Pressure Test. R/D Slickline Equipment.

        3.  Install Proper two-way Check Valve (TWCV- 2 31/64” “H” Cameron) inside tubing hanger
       & pressure test it.

             Note:

                    -   Existing Tubing Hanger: 10" x 5 1/2" x 2 7/8" EUE (VETCO GRAY) W/2-31/64"
             Two Way Check Valve
                    -   Existing Tubing: 166 JTS 2 7/8" Tubing, 6.5#, L80, EUE, 1600.8 m as Kick Off
                   String
                    -   Prepare suitable cross over for drill pipes and landing joint to connect to tubing
                hanger for pulling the completion string.

        4. Remove kill line from X-mass tree, do not disconnect kill line from Annulus until the BOP
          stack is installed. Keep the well full from Annulus side. Closely monitor the well condition.
        5.  While circulate cross wellhead, N/D X-Mass tree and bonnet, check tubing hanger
           thread.

             Note:
                    -   Check, measure and report tubing hanger neck size.
                    -  Clean and wash tubing hanger threads.
                    -  N/D X-Mass Tree in day light.
                    -   Circulate cross well head to keep well full while N/D, N/U and trip.

        6. N/U 13 5/8”-5K BOP stack and Rotating Head, perform BOP test. do not forget to open
           tubing head spool outlets during test.
        7.  Rig up 2 7/8” EUE tubing handling tools, make up 2 7/8” EUE x 4 ½” IF landing joint and
         screw in tubing hanger.
        8.  close upper pipe rams & perform pressure test all rams below upper rams to 3000 psi,
          loose tie down screws, pressure test glands to 3000 psi.
        9.  Circulate & balance mud weight, POOH 2 7/8” Kick Off string with Accessories from 1600.8
      m to surface & L/D Jt by Jt, while checking all equipment and string as per completion
          engineer instruction.

             Note:
                    -   Before pulling the completion string, observe the well situation not to be under
                     significant levels of losses.
                    -   In case of shortage of proper landing joint or cross over  it is recommended to
                 prepare 2 7/8” spear / taper tap or overshot (short catch) on well site to recover
                 Completion string.
                    -  Check top threads of TBG hanger to ensure engagement with retrieving Tools

                  before pulling the string. Record and report condition of TBG HGR threads.
                    -   In case of existence of debris on Tubing Hanger Threads, Acid may be used to
                  clean the threads with coordination of Supervisor and Drilling office.
                    -   While POOH, circulate across THS and monitor the well to be full.
                    -   While POOH Completion String, Control the Pulling speed to avoid swabbing. If
                observed any significant abnormal drags, take care not to overpull the string and
                  also not to put the well on swab. In case, Report to office immediately.
                    -   Prepare cross-over of T.Hanger top thread to DP connection.

   4.3 Clean Out Trip to 5” Liner

        1. P/U & M/U 8 1/2" Clean out BHA including 8 1/2" J.Mill (R.Bit) , M/U Dps and DCs Jt by
             Jt, RIH, Clean the well to 5” L.Lap, Tag L.Lap, Circulate Bottoms up. POOH and L/D BHA
         and Mill (R.Bit).
        2. P/U & M/U 4 1/8" Clean out BHA including 4 1/8" J.Mill (R.Bit) , M/U 2 7/8”, 3 ½” and 5”
        Dps and DCs Jt by Jt, RIH, Clean the well to 2200 m, Circulate Bottoms up. POOH and
         L/D BHA and Mill (R.Bit).

             Note:
                    -   Circulate bottoms up every 40 Joints.
                    -   During RIH, take care to avoid string stuck by Probable unknown Obstacles In the
                     well. inform NISOC Representative and drilling office in case of any abnormal
                   event.
                    -   In case of observing Severe losses, Spot LCM Pills. If not cured, POOH to surface
               and RIH with open-end string to Spot Magneset Pill to cure the losses (detailed
                Procedure will be issued at due time).
                    -     If Severe losses not cured by LCM  Pill, perform magneset plug as level-off
                 procedure issued at due time.

   4.4 Cement Plug Back

        1. Based on well Loss condition, in case of Static Well Condition, RIH W/ 2 7/8” & 3.5” OEDP
          Followed by 5" DP to 2200, mix & pump 52 bbls cement as balance plug (Expected top of
         cement @ 1830 m), POOH to 1500 m, flush string, CBU, POOH to 1000 m wait on cement,
          RIH, tag & confirm top of cement, OBS well, POOH to surface.

             Note:
                    -   In case of Severe losses, Perform the cement plug as Level-off plug according to
                 procedure issued in due time.
                    -  The volume of cement will be adjusted as per well & loss condition in due time.
                    -     It may need to repeat the cement plug based on hole condition.

             General Note:
                    -  When performing a cementing plug operation, POOH to about 150 m above
                 expected top of cement, Flush the string, clean the well, then POOH to upper safe
                Depth and WOC to get hard.

        2. RIH W/ 8 1/2" R.Bit, tag top of cement, dress cement to 1860 m, Report Cement Quality.
            circulate out Hi-Vis pill to clean the well, pressure test Existing 9 5/8” casing to max 2000
            psi, OBS well, Displace the Mud to heavy mud appropriate as barrier on top of cement
           Plug, POOH to surface. (MW required to be displaced on top of plug would be determined
           at due time)

   4.5 Changing T.H.S

        1. P/U & M/U 9 5/8" Scraper with same BHA and 8 1/2" R.Bit, RIH To 30 m, scrap depth of
         30 m for setting RTTS packer to change T.H.S.
        2.  Circulate Bottoms Up, POOH to Surf, L/D 8 1/2" Bit and Scraper.
        3. P/U & M/U 9 5/8" RTTS with setting tools. RIH to 30 m. Set RTTS at 30 m and test sealing,
      POOH RTTS Setting Tools.
        4. N/D BOP Stack.
        5.  L/D Old Tubing Head Spool (13 5/8” * 11”-3K, X(P) Series).
        6. N/U and Install New Tubing Head Spool 13 5/8” * 11”-3K, V(H/G)-3K Series. Energize
          P-Seal. Pressure test with 2500 Psi (Max 80% of Casing Collapse Pressure 9 5/8”, 43.5
         PPF, N80, considering De-rated pipe.)

             Note:

                    -  When L/D old T.H.S, check and report the condition of old 9 5/8” Casing neck to
               be appropriate for New T.H.S installation.
                    -   Scraping of Whipstock packer setting depth could be done at this step.

        7. N/D 13 5/8”-5K Rotating Head and N/U BOP Stack and Test Stack and related lines as
        NISOC Policy.

             Note:
                    -  11”-3K * 13 5/8”-5K DSA Flange would be used above T.H.S to install the BOP
                   stack.
        8. P/U RTTS setting tools. RIH to 30 m. Screw RTTS at 30 m, Release and POOH RTTS to
           surface. L/D RTTS and Setting tools.

   4.6 Window Opening and Sidetracking

        1. RIH 8 1/2" bit (J.Mill) with 9 5/8" scraper and Recommended BHA of /whipstock services
           to 1860 m, scrap whipstock setting depths twice. circulate out Hi-Vis pill, OBS the well,
      POOH to surface & L/D Bit (Mill) & scraper.

             Note:
                    -   Tally the pipe while running in or out of the well to get an accurate measurement
                    to the Packer setting depth.
                    -   Perform Well Control Drill prior to window Opening.
        2. R/U Logging Equipment, Run CBL/VDL/CCL log to 1860 m to determine the cement
            quality and locate exact Coupling depths at window opening depth. POOH to Surface.

             Note:
                    -  Use the log result to evaluate the cement quality at window opening depth and
                  not to open the window at casing coupling. Window should be opened at intervals
                   with good cement quality.

        3.  Prepare and M/U whipstock Assembly with Gyro UBHO Sub in assembly. RIH W/9 5/8"
          Hydraulic Set Permanent Whipstock Assembly (with 8-1/2" Mill Assembly - suitable for 9
           5/8" casing 53.5#, N80, BTS Thread), Orient whipstock and adjust tool face using Gyro
          according to Planned Azimuth, set packer as per whipstock services procedure and test
         same.

             Note:
                    -  While Running Gyro, Check Well trajectory F/Surface to Window Opening
               Depth Each 50 meters.
                    -  Use Gyro to orient whip face at required azimuth of directional plan.
                    -   Detailed Whipstock Setting and window opening procedures would be issued in
               due time.
                    -  Depth of 1840 m for window opening, would be evaluated to be a proper point
                 according to procedures.
                    -   Equal Window depth of main hole had been drilled W/125 PCF water Base Mud.
              Window opening would be done by 125 PCF Mud to ensure enough overbalance
               and maintain well stability in directional condition.

        4.  Displace well with 125 PCF S.Saturated Heavy mud, OBS well, Start milling casing and
         opening window@ 1840 m MD. observe the well while opening window, Dress window
          several times and drill 4 meters of formation.
        5. CBU. POOH mill assembly to surface.

             Note:
                    -   circulate out Hi-Vis pill during milling operation as per procedure. Make sure the
                    well to be clean out of milling Debris.
                    -  Use Ditch Magnet to take the debris out of the Circulating fluid.
                    -   While Milling Window, take formation samples precisely to make sure getting
              away enough from existing wellbore. Adjust MW According to Well Condition.

        8 1/2” Directional Drilling

         1. P/U & M/U 8 1/2" Roller Cone bit and Directional BHA as Per BHA Program (included
         MWD), RIH to Bottom.

              Note:
                      -  Use Roller Cone bit as first Run entering the window to avoid PDC Bit early dulling
                and better controlling the azimuth. Drill to dull the bit.
                      -  Meanwhile Drilling to section TD, Adjust MW based on hole Condition.
                      -  GS formation of main hole had been drilled W/105-129 PCF Mud.
                      -     If Gachsaran Mud weight exceeds the maximum value used for 7” liner design,
                        liner design should be rechecked with new maximum mud weight.

         2. Commence directional drilling toward N99 degrees Azimuth to maximum 25 degrees
             inclination Angle at 2058.6 m MD as M.B.U.A, according to directional Program, Cont.
            Directional Drilling (Keep Section) to 2120 m MD (Section TD).

              Note:

                      -     If BHA Tendency was not suitable to Hold the inclination, make a trip to change
                   the BHA.

       7” Liner Running, Cementing and L.Lap test

        5. R/U Liner Running Equipment. Run 400 m of 7" Liner (29#, L80, SD:6.15”, PJE) F/1720
         T/2120 m & hang same. Exact Liner Running Procedure will be issued in due time.
        6.  Release Setting tool, Hold in Place, perform 7" Liner Cement Job and POOH to Safe depth
          (about 1550 m), flush the string and circulate to clean the well, POOH to 500 m, close the
           well while WOC and Monitor Well Press. WOC to get Hard.

             Note: Run 7” Liner As following

                       -   7" Float Shoe, 29 ppf, L-80, SD, PJE, PDC Drillable.
                       -  Two Joints 7" Liner, 29 ppf, L-80, SD, PJE, Seamless
                       -   7" Float Collar, 29 ppf, L-80, SD, PJE, PDC Drillable.
                       -  Two Joint 7" Liner, 29 ppf, L-80, SD, PJE, Seamless
                       -   7" Latch Down Collar, 29 ppf, L-80, SD, PJE, Landing (Latch Down) Collar, PDC Drillable.
                        ▪   1720-2120: 400 m, 7" Liner, 29 ppf, L-80, PJE, Seamless
                       -   7" Non-Rotating Liner Hanger- Hydraulic Set, 29 ppf, L-80, PJE

        7. POOH to Surface, L/D setting tools.
        8. P/U 8 1/2" R.Bit, RIH to top of cement. Clean Out and Tag Liner lap. Report the cement
           Quality above L.Lap (if Any). Perform L.Lap Pressure test According to below note.

             Note:
                    -     If more than 50 meters of hard cement observed above liner lap, no need to
                 perform pressure test of L.Lap.
                    -      If hard Cement not observed above L.Lap, clean out inside the liner to top of LDC
                 according to next steps, perform pressure test of L.Lap with 2000 Psi pressure, if
                    leaking, Perform Injectivity test and proceed to inject Cement.  if not leaking,
                 perform L.Lap Inflow Test according to next steps.

        9.  Circ Btm`s Up, POOH to Surf, B/O Bit.
       10. RIH W/ 6 1/8" R.Bit While P/U 3 1/2" and 5'' DPs as tandem BHA. Drill out Pack Off
          Bushing, clean inside liner to top of LDC. Report the cement quality if any.

             Note:
                    -      If Hard Cement not observed above LDC, drill out LDC and 2-3 meter of Cement
                  below, if hard cement not observed below LDC, Inform Drilling office before taking
                any action.
       11. Circulate, Clean the Hole. POOH to 5 m above liner Lap, Displace the well with water
          stepwise, perform inflow test of 7" liner lap. Monitor the well pressure for any increase. If
        OK, POOH to surface, L/D Bit and BHA.  if L.Lap was leaking, take injectivity test and
         proceed for cement injection according to procedure.

             Note:
                    -  Cement injection procedure would be issued in due time.
       12. RIH W/ 6 1/8" R.Bit While P/U 3 1/2" and 5'' DPs as tandem BHA (Including Junk Sub).
       13. Drill out 7” Liner LDC, F.Collar and cement to 5 meter of above Shoe. Circulate, Clean the
          Hole. Work on Junk Sub.

             Note:
                    -   Perform Well Control Drill and H2S Drill prior to drill out of 7” Liner shoe track
               and entering the Asmari Formation.
                    -  Ensure good performance of Rotating head before start of 6 1/8” hole section
               and entering Asmari formation.

               Magneset before start of 6 1/8” hole section and entering Asmari formation.
       14. Circ BTM’s Up, POOH to 1000 m, displace the well with water, observe the well, if Static,
        RIH to BTM, Displace the well with water Stepwise, observe the well.
       15. Circ BTM's Up, POOH to Surf & L/D Bit and Junk Sub.

      POOH Logging Equipment.

         observe the well, if OK, drill 2 meter of formation, Displace the well with New Mud. Observe
          the well. Circ BTM's Up, POOH to Surf & L/D Bit and Junk Sub.

   4.8 6 1/8” Hole Section Sequence of Operation

       General

                                               5’’ Liner Shoe @ 2416.5, L.Lap @ 2000 m, Section TD: 2449.4 m
    Casing/Liner Point
                                        (Exact liner shoe depth will be determined based on log result evaluations)

    Formation               Cap Rock, Asmari

                               Stuck Pipe, Tight Holes, Well Flow, Mud Losses coupled with gas
    Risks & Hole Problem
                                      flow, Gas Cut Mud, Swabbing and Well Flow, H2S Exposure
    Deviation Control       MWD

                        Open Hole logs: Full suite and Image Log (Asmari Fm)
    Logging
                          Cased Hole logs: SBT-VDL-CCL-GR Inside 5'' LNR

                                        EMULSION MUD

                  MW (pcf)                     ± 55.5

                                      Vis. (sec/qt)                  40 - 50

     Drilling Fluid Parameters    PV (cp)                   ALAP (4-7)

                       YP (lb/100ft²)                 ± 9-12

                             API FL (ml/ 30min)          NC

                       PH                         <8

                                              6 1/8” PDC BIT + PDM

                     GPM                         250-400
     Drilling Parameters
                    WOB                          5-15

                          SURF. RPM                   25-35

       6 1/8” Directional Drilling
   1.  After Displacing the well with New Mud. Observe the well. If OK, Drill Formation to 2148 m.
   2. Circ BTM's Up, POOH to Surf & L/D Bit and Junk Sub.
   3. P/U & M/U 6 1/8" PDC bit and directional BHA (Keep Section) as Per BHA Program (included
     MWD), RIH to Bottom.
   4. Continue 6 1/8” Directional drilling Curve Section to 2449.4 m MD. (Well TD). Use Acid
       Soluble LCM Pills Periodically to cure Losses.
      Note:
                    -   In case of severe losses, perform magneset plug as level-off procedure to cure
                  losses (Contingency time considered for loss Curing) / If ROP or BHA Tendency
                       is not acceptable make a trip to change the Bit/ BHA. Whether else, continue
                        drilling.
                    -  Meanwhile Drilling to section TD, Adjust MW based on hole Condition.
                    -   Rotating Head should be used while drilling Asmari formation to TD.

                       Well: BH-144, W.O.2, S.T.2
                       BiBi Hakimeh Oil Field

Page 71 of 151                  Doc. No: BH-15-SM-110-DR-DM-1789-D02                        Rev:02

                    -   Trip Tank should be fully in service meanwhile drilling and Trips.
                    -   Since Drilling through Gas Cap of the Reservoir, Follow Gas Cap Drilling Policy
                  (section 13.2) from Asmari formation to TD.
                    -   Adjust MW based on hole condition.
                    -   Consider the risk of mud losses followed by well flow while drilling through
                Asmari Formation.

   5. Perform Wiper TRIP and Condition TRIP Prior to Reservoir Evaluation Logging. Circulate till
      shaker clean. POOH To Surf & L/D Bit & BHA.
             Note:
                    -  Use Acid Soluble LCM Pill Periodically to cure Losses.
                    -   In case of severe losses not cured by pumping LCM, perform magneset plug as
                      level-off procedure to cure losses (Contingency time considered for loss Curing).
                  Level off procedure would be issued in due time.
                    -   During Tripping out of the hole, Observe the well at shoe and also when tripping
                  out the BHA.
                    -   Adjust mud weight according to hole condition.
                    -   During trips, while at surface, driller should check BHA.
                    -   During POOH, Observe the well condition for any loss/gain. Monitor gas content
                    of drilling fluid. Any changes in volume should be reported.
                    -  Choke line and choke manifold must be lined up for SOFT SHUT-IN. (Soft Shut
                      in Procedure described in section 13.1, Well Control)
                    -  Ensure that adequate fishing tools are available for this section.
                    -   Closely Observe the well (Loss/Gain Condition), monitor gas content of returned
                       drilling fluid, and report any intense change in volumes.
                    -   Since drilling through reservoir section, all H2S service equipment should be
                 checked, be in service and all rig personnel trained properly.

   6. Test BOP Stack and all Related Lines as NISOC POLICY.
   7. R/U Logging Equipment. Hold PJSM.
   8. Run Full Suite log of Asmari Fm.
   9. P/U Last 6 1/8” R.Bit and Directional BHA, Perform Condition Trip Prior To Image log.
       Circulate and clean the hole, POOH To Surf & R/B Bit & BHA
   10. Run Image Log of Asmari Fm. POOH logging Equipment.
             Note:
                    -   Shooting nipple should be installed before logging operation.
                    -  Use MWD results For Open Hole data.

       Magneset plug Back
    11. P/U Last 6 1/8” R.Bit and BHA, Perform Condition Trip Prior To Set Magneset Plug inside
       open hole (based on log result).
              Note:
                      -     If Severe losses, pump magneset plug as level-off according to procedure issued
                       in due time.
    12.  Circulate and clean the hole, POOH to Surf & R/B Bit & BHA.
    13. RIH W/ OEDP (2 7/8” & 3 1/2" DP followed 5" DP) to BTM, Break Circulation Prior to
       pumping the Magneset plug.
    14.  Mix & Pump 10 bbl. Magneset Plug & Displace W/ Mud as Balanced plug (in case of Static
         well condition). Expected Top of Magneset if NL/NG Condition exist would be 2366 m. if well
         condition was not static, perform the magneset plug as level off procedure to cure losses and
        plug the well according to procedure issued in due time.
              Note:
                      -   Contingency time considered to cure the losses or perform several Magneset
                    plugs.
    15. POOH to 2250 m, Flush the string, circulate to clean the well, POOH to 1600 m and W.O
       Magneset, RIH Back & Tag Top of Magneset, Test Consistency by Apply Weight & Pump out.
     POOH to surface.
              Note:
                      -   Exact Magneset pumping and Plug back Programs, will be issued in due time.

    16. RIH W/ 6 1/8" R.Bit to tag Top of Magneset and D/O to get Hard Magneset. Dress the plug
         to required depth of 5" liner shoe based on log results (2416.5 m as initial 5” shoe depth, exact
        depth will be determined based on log results).

       5” Liner Running and Cementing
   17. Perform Condition TRIP, Prior To Run 5" Liner. Circulate to Clean the Hole. Spot 40 bbl. Hi-
       Vis Pill @ Bottom. POOH to Surface. Check top Rams to be 5".
   18.  Test BOP Stack and all Related Lines as NISOC POLICY.
   19. Hold PJSM.
   20. R/U Liner Running Equipment. Run 416.5 m of 5" Liner, 18#, L-80, TPFJ-II F/2000 T/2416.5
    m & hang same. Exact 5” liner shoe depth will be determined after log results evaluation. Liner
      Running Procedure will be issued in due time.
             Note: Run 5” Liner As following
                   ▪   5" Float Shoe, 18 ppf, L80, TPFJ-II , PDC Drillable.
                   ▪  Two Joints 5" Liner, 18 ppf, L80, TPFJ-II, Seamless
                   ▪   5" Float Collar, 18 ppf, L80, TPFJ-II, PDC Drillable.
                   ▪  Two Joint 5" Liner, 18 ppf, L80, TPFJ-II, Seamless
                   ▪   5" Latch Down Collar, 18 ppf, L80, TPFJ-II, Landing (Latch Down) Collar, PDC
                            Drillable.
                     o  2000-2416.5: 416.5 m, 5" Liner, 18 ppf, L80, TPFJ-II, Seamless
                   ▪   5" Non-Rotating Liner Hanger- Hydraulic Set, 18 ppf, L80, TPFJ-II
   21. Release the setting tool, Hold in Place, Perform 5" Liner Cement Job. POOH to Safe depth
      (1700 m), Flush the string, circulate to clean the well, POOH to 1200 m and close the well
       while WOC and Monitor Well Press. WOC to get Hard.
   22. POOH to Surface, L/D setting tools.
   23. P/U 6 1/8" R.Bit, RIH to top of cement. Clean Out and Tag Liner lap. Report the cement
       Quality above L.Lap (if Any). Perform L.Lap Pressure test According to below note.
             Note:
                    -      If more than 50 meters of hard cement observed above liner lap, no need to
                 perform pressure test of L.Lap.
                    -     If hard Cement not observed above L.Lap, clean out inside the liner to top of LDC
                 according to next steps, perform pressure test of L.Lap (2000 Psi Pressure), If
                    leaking, Perform Injectivity test and proceed to inject Cement.  if not leaking
                proceed to perform L.Lap Dry test.
   24. Circ Btm`s Up, POOH to Surf, L/D Bit and BHA.
   25. P/U and M/U 4 1/8" Clean out BHA. RIH W/ 4 1/8" PDC Bit (Mill) While P/U 2 7/8”, 3 1/2”
      and 5'' DPs as tandem BHA. Drill out Pack Off Bushing & Cont. Clean Out Inside 5” Liner to
       top of LDC. Report Cement Quality (if any) to make sure about cement Hardness before Liner
       lap dry test. POOH To surface and L/D Bit and BHA.
             Note:
                    -     If hard cement not observed above LDC, D/O LDC and 2-3 meters cement below
                                    till get hard cement. if Hard cement not observed below LDC, report to office to
              make decision before proceeding to next step.
   26. P/U & M/U 7" Scraper with 6 1/8” Clean out BHA and 6 1/8" R.Bit, RIH to 20 m above 5”
       Liner Lap. Scrap the packer setting Depth several times.
   27.  Circulate Bottoms Up, POOH to Surf, L/D 6 1/8" R.Bit and Scraper.
   28. P/U Dry Test Equip & Run to 20 m Above L-lap.
   29.  Perform 5'' L.Lap Dry test as per procedure.

             Note:

   41. N/D 13 5/8"-5K Rotating Head and BOP Stack.
   42. N/UP New X-mas Tree (11" * 7 1/16" 3K – V(H/G) Series) & Pressure Test All.
             Note:
                    -   Well Completion Program would be issued in due time.

   7.4 Well killing Fluid

        ▪  Based on well pressure it is recommended to use drill water with 62.4 pcf weight as killing
              fluid.
        ▪  Before operation on rig tanks should be fill by water.
        ▪   Appropriate amount of LCM should be available onboard.
        ▪  Polymeric Hi-Vis Pill will be pump to clean the well after killing it.
        ▪  Before pumping balanced cement plug 50bbl Hi-Vis Pill recommended to spot in desired
          depth
        ▪   Hi-Vis Pill Mixing Procedure:
              ▪    Fill up the tank with required amount of brine
              ▪   Adjust the pH to 10 with caustic soda
              ▪  Add XC-polymer 1.5-2pbb (a 25kg sack must be mixed in 15minutes to avoid
                 formation of fish eyes)

     Mud Discussion

   Gachsaran formation totally consists of a repetition of Anhydrite, grey and red marl as well as salt
   and some limestone beds.
     It is necessary to adjust mud weight and mud properties as hole condition dictate. Some tight
   hole, well flow, severe losses and low ROP maybe experienced in this hole section. The drilling
    fluid engineer will advise the drilling supervisor of any extended circulation or sweep requirement.
     It uses clarified viscosifier to provide viscosity and carrying capacity and starch to reduce mud
     filtrate.

        Interval Mud Discussion

   Saturated Salt Muds are primarily used to prevent excessive hole enlargement while drilling
   massive salt beds. They can also be used to reduce dispersion and hydration of shales and clays.
    Salt and Salt Saturated Muds are used to prevent excessive hole enlargement while drilling
   massive salt beds. They can also be used to reduce dispersion and hydration of shales and clays.
   Solids content (percent by volume) should be maintained within a desired range by mechanical
   removal or dilution with saturated salt water to prevent unacceptable increases in gel strengths
   and viscosity. The chloride content of saturated salt muds is 188,000 mg/l at saturation. As the
   temperature of the mud increases, more salt is able to go into solution. This means that a fluid
   which is saturated under surface conditions may not be saturated in down hole temperatures and
   can cause substantial washout in a salt zone due to increased salt solubility. Control of the pH of
   saturated salt muds varies widely. Saturated salt muds require larger additions of caustic soda to
   maintain a pH in Salt Saturate Muds.

       Mixing Procedure

   Mixing Procedure for Salt Polymer Mud

      1.  Fill the pit with calculated amount of Drill water 0.97 bbl/bbl.
      2. Add 0.5-1.0 lb/bbl of Soda Ash to treat out any hardness.
      3. Add 0.4-0.6 lb./bbl Lime to raise pH up to 9.5.
      4. Add 110-125 lb/bbl Salt to get desired MW and salt saturated.
      5. Add 12-14 lb/bbl Starch Green to get desired fluid loss.
      6. Add Barite As needed.

         Drilling Issues

   Stuck pipe

    In case of stuck pipe, Pip-Lax pill can be spotted around stuck area.

   Rheology & Hydraulics

    Dilution with fresh mud and/or premixes should be made periodically to ensure good rheological
   properties and minimize solids build-up; Rheology should be maintained in specification in order
    to minimize the effects of ECD on the formation.

                                                                                       Page 96 of 151

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                    Workover Program For
                       Well: BH-144, W.O.2, S.T.2
                       BiBi Hakimeh Oil Field

Page 97 of 151                  Doc. No: BH-15-SM-110-DR-DM-1789-D02                        Rev:02

   Attention should be paid to pump rates and hydraulic models to achieve optimum flow regime.
   Hence avoid washing out hole sections that may lead to ledges.

   Solids Control Management

   Dress Shakers with 140 ‐175 mesh size screen and mud cleaner with 230 mesh.
   Ensure shakers are running immediately prior to and during all periods of circulation. Use all
   the solids control equipment available on the rig site. The combination of linear motion s h a
       l e shaker with centrifuge proved best results. Centrifuge should be run at all the time at high
   flow rate on barite recovery mode  to remove low gravity  solids and keep mud weight &
   properties in range.

  Down Hole Losses
   Heavy and Complete losses are not expected while drilling this section. The concentration of
  LCM will vary according to losses. In the event of seepage losses, treat system with a finer
   grade of LCM at 10 – 15 lb/bbl. In the event of moderate to high rate of well bore loss, prepare
    pills at different graded concentrations and spot over thief zone. The concentration may vary
   between 20 – 70 lb/bbl. Ensure that the driller displace the LCM pill out of the string prior to
   shutting down the mud pumps

          Lost Circulation Strategy
         ➢ Maintain sufficient stock of LCM materials (compatible with reservoir) on board is
                necessary
         ➢ The concentration of LCM will vary according to the amount of losses.
         ➢ Keep MW in a minimum range.

       Engineering Concerns

   Hole Cleaning

    Cleaning cuttings from a vertical hole has been covered extensively by numerous investigators.
   Hole cleaning in a vertical hole is essentially a matter of overcoming the slip velocity of the cuttings
    with sufficient annular velocity and carrying capacity of the drilling fluid. Although any velocity
    greater than the settling velocity of the largest cutting will theoretically lift all the cuttings to surface
    eventually, too low an annular velocity will lead to an undesirable high concentration of cuttings
    in the annulus. Experience has shown that cuttings concentrations more than 5% by volume
   cause tight hole or stuck pipe when circulation is stopped.

   Down Hole Losses

   Heavy losses are expected in this section due to highly fractured reservoir & porous media.
   The LCM pumping strategy are as below:

         Seepage Losses (25 ppb LCM in WBM):
                      • 10 ppb CaCO3 Chips(M)
                      • 5 ppb CaCO3 Chips(F)
                      • 5 ppb Oyster Shell(M)
                      • 5 ppb Oyster Shell(F)

            Partial Losses (40 ppb LCM in WBM):
                      • 5 ppb CaCO3 Chips(C)
                      • 10 ppb CaCO3 Chips(M)
                      • 10 ppb CaCO3 Chips(F)
                      • 10 ppb Oyster Shell(M)
                      • 5 ppb Oyster Shell(F)

         Heavy Losses (55 ppb LCM in WBM):
                      • 10 ppb CaCO3 Chips(C)
                      • 15 ppb CaCO3 Chips(M)
                      • 15 ppb CaCO3 Chips(F)
                      • 10 ppb Oyster Shell(M)
                      • 5 ppb Oyster Shell(F)

            be need based on well condition.

         Balanced Cement / Magneset Plug Program

       Before Open the window balanced cement plug will be set in 9 5/8” casing to isolate the
        existing open hole section form new well and also a magneset cement plug will be set in
     new 6 1/8” (TD) open hole in Asmari Formation. The detail program of plug pumping will
      be issued during operation based on operation condition.

      Magneset plugs may be used to cure and control well losses. In case of Severe losses,
       the plug will be pumped as level-off plug.

      8.1 Bit & Hydraulic Program

12. General   Quality,   Health,   Safety  and
  Environmental Requirements

 The overall strategic objectives of HSE management during drilling, completion, clean up and
 testing operations are:
    •  To ensure the health and safety of personnel.
    •  To safeguard the integrity of wells, facilities and installations
    •  Prevent adverse effects on the environment and third parties.
    •  Zero Rating
              Spill to the ground
          Dropped objects
           Well control occurrence
    •  H2S, Diverter and Well Control Certification and drill procedures
    •   Daily safety meetings and weekly fire and emergency drill
    •  Housekeeping daily
    •  HSE reports should be sent to client at monthly, weakly basis and after rig
        release.

 HSE will focus on:
    •  On / off loading materials to prevent drop of materials and equipment
    •  Communication to all personnel. Enforce communication to create involvement for all
        personnel based on acting as a ‘Team’. Radio communication for entire drilling unit and
       headphone for specific work in areas of high background noise.
    •   Pre-job safety meetings start of each crew change.
    •   All crew trained H2S. Regular H2S drill will be held.
    •   Derrick will be inspected continuous basis to notify loose objects and equipment
    •   All crew should report any non-conformances.
    •  As part of the contractor selection process, evaluate the contractor's management of
     HSE during well construction activities and ensure that the HSE-MS is suitable and
          sufficient.

 At a glance, the HSE important activity and plans is available as below table:

             Safety Meeting / Drills                     Frequency

                 Rig Induction                      Immediately on arrival to rig
            Pre-Tour Safety Meetings                At the start of each 12hr Tour
             Pre-Job Toolbox talks                   Prior to specific rig operations
          Daily Service Company Meeting                          Daily
            Weekly Safety Meeting                        Weekly
                 Fire /Abandon Drills                         Weekly
             Well Control / BOP Drills                       Weekly
         HSE Training Meetings                         Monthly

                     Well: BH-144, W.O.2, S.T.2
                      BiBi Hakimeh Oil Field

13. Well Control and H2S Safety Measures

13.1 Well Control

(1) Recording Reference

All BOP tests, drills, function tests, any malfunctions, repair or maintenance to the mud system
and well control equipment shall be recorded in the IADC daily reports and shall be signed by
both the Drilling Contractor's Tool Pusher and Drilling Supervisor on the well site. They shall
also be recorded in the Daily Drilling Report.

Note:

Flow Meter should be Used During Workover and Drilling Operations Until Rig Released.

(2) Flow Check Procedure

To avoid a kick situation, it is recommended that a flow check be conducted at specific times
during the drilling operation.
        •  While drilling (any warning sign)
        •   Prior to tripping the drill string
        •  While tripping (any warning sign)
        •  While out of hole(continuously)
        •   Trip out:

       ➢   After pulling 5% of the drill pipe
       ➢  At the midpoint depth of the wellbore
       ➢   Prior to pulling the first stand of drill collars
       ➢   After all the drill string is out of hole.

        •   Trip in:

       ➢  Upon reaching surface casing shoe
       ➢  At midpoint of the wellbore

(3) Primary Control Reference

It is the responsibility of the Drilling Contractor to keep the hole full at all times by using all the
available equipment. The Drilling Supervisor shall monitor that the correct preventive practices
are being implemented and/or conducted at all times.

Extreme care shall be taken to monitor mud volume, drilling breaks and gas cut mud.

The MACP (Maximum allowable casing pressure) shall be confirmed by the Drilling Supervisor,
either after setting each new casing string or, whenever the density of the drilling mud changes.

The MACP shall be clearly written on a kick control sheet that will be posted near the choke

                                                                                   Page 138 of 151

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                   Workover Program For
                     Well: BH-144, W.O.2, S.T.2
                      BiBi Hakimeh Oil Field

 Page 139 of 151             Doc. No: BH-15-SM-110-DR-DM-1789-D02                 Rev:02

control panel.

The predetermined distance at which the Tool Joint is to be pulled above the rotary should be
posted on rig floor near the BOP control panel.

The Driller is responsible for carefully measuring and recording the slow pump pressure.
Awareness of these values is an important element in killing operations, in order to avoid
formation breakdown.

Take a measure and record the slow pump pressure at three different flow rates with all pumps
at the following times:

         1)  Once per tour.
         2)  Whenever changes occur in the mud density/rheology.
         3)  Whenever changes occur in the dimension and characteristics of the string, BHA,
                jet size, jet plugged or jet lost, etc.
         4)  Secondary Control Reference

       Any time a drilling break is noticed, drilling shall stop and a static monitoring of the
         well shall be carried out. Space-out for all Pipe Rams and Annular Preventer must be
       known to the Driller at all times to keep tool joints clear of annular preventer and rams.

         5)  Kick Detection Procedure While Drilling

              If there is an indication of a possible kick occurring, the Driller shall follow the kick
         detection procedures outlined below:

        Stop drilling with the pump on→Pick up top drive to a predetermined space-out
        position→Shut off the pumps and check for well flow by visual examination on the bell
         nipple or flow line.

             If the well is not flowing:

        •  Immediately notify the rig manager, Drilling Supervisor and Tool pusher.
        •  The Drilling Supervisor will decide whether to circulate bottoms up or resume
               drilling. The penetration rate will be limited as long as the cause of the abnormal
             situation is undetermined. Always be aware that some hydrocarbons (gas or oil cut
         mud) may rise to the surface. Ensure that the proper monitoring and safety
          systems all in place.

             If the well is flowing:

        •  Close the well as per the Shut-in Procedure.
        •  Immediately notify the Wellsite Drilling Supervisor, rig manager and Tool pusher.

(4) Soft Shut-in Procedure and Line Up

During operations the circuit has to be lined up as follows:
        •  Manuel valve on choke line is opened
        •   Hydraulic valve on choke line has to be closed
        •  The choke of choke manifold has to be opened

                                                                                   Page 139 of 151

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                   Workover Program For
                     Well: BH-144, W.O.2, S.T.2
                      BiBi Hakimeh Oil Field

 Page 140 of 151             Doc. No: BH-15-SM-110-DR-DM-1789-D02                 Rev:02

        •   All the valves leading to the separator passing by the choke (downstream valves)
          have to be in open positions.
        •  Beside these valves, all the valves have to be in closed position.

    Soft Shut-in procedure while drilling

      The procedure is performed as follows:
          -  if there is any kick indication, stop rotation, pick up off bottom and space out (Tool
         joint one meter above rotary table to avoid getting the next tool joint on the pipe rams)
          - Stop pumps, perform flow check. If the well is flowing then:
          - Open choke line valve at the BOP stack (Called HCR)
          - Close annular BOP
          - Close Choke
          - Read and Record pressures and times. Check pit volume gain in order to prepare the
           kill sheet
          - If the gas is migrating, control the wellbore pressures during the shut in.

    Soft Shut-in procedure while tripping

              If there is any kick indication, stop tripping immediately. Here, two different situations
       can be identified:

         a) The well is flowing, then the shut in is proceeded as follows:
            - Set the string on the slips
            - Install a fully opening safety valve in open position. Close the valve once is installed
            - Open choke line valve at BOP stack (HCR valve)
            - Close annular BOP
            - Close choke.
            - Read and record pressures and times, check pit volumes
              It depends on the situation to weather to start killing procedures or to strip back to the
        bottom.
            - If the stripping is faced, then, Stab IBOP (Grey valve)
            - Open the fully opening safety valve
            - Reduce the annular pressure and start stripping the string to the bottom

        b) The well is not flowing:
            - Set the string on the slips
            - Install IBOP (grey valve or non-return valve)
            - Run back in the hole with controlling the volumes, if any anomalies are noticed then
       proceed to the stripping. Once on bottom, circulate annular volume and evaluate the
          situation.

(5) Hard Shut-in Procedure and Line Up

       During operations the circuit has to be aligned as follows:
        •  Manuel valve on choke line is opened
        •   Hydraulic valve on choke line has to be closed
        •  The choke-on-choke manifold has to be closed
        •   All the valves leading to the separator passing by the choke (downstream valves)
          have to be in open positions.

                                                                                   Page 140 of 151

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                   Workover Program For
                     Well: BH-144, W.O.2, S.T.2
                      BiBi Hakimeh Oil Field

 Page 141 of 151             Doc. No: BH-15-SM-110-DR-DM-1789-D02                 Rev:02

        •  Beside these valves, all the valves have to be in closed position.

   Hard shut-in procedure while drilling
         - if there is any kick indication, stop rotary, pick up off bottom and space out ( Tool joint
      one meter above rotary table to avoid getting the next tool joint on the pipe rams)
          - Stop pumps, perform flow check. If the well is flowing then:
          - Close annular or pipe rams
          - Open choke line HCR valve
          - Start plotting the trend of pressures, drill pipe pressure and casing pressure, note also
       the gain.

   Hard shut-in procedure while tripping

If any kick indication is noticed, the tripping has to be ceased immediately and the next steps
have to be performed. Two situations ca be faced:

       a) The well is flowing, then the shut in is proceeded as follows:
        •  Set the drilling string on the slips
        •   Install the fully opening safety valve in opened position
        •  Close the safety valve
        •  Close the annular BOP
        •  Open the HCR valve on the choke line
        •  Record the pressures with the time and the gain volume

       b) The well is not flowing:
        •  Set the drilling string on the slips
        •   Install the IBOP (grey valve or the non-return valve)
        •   Trip back in the hole with controlling the volumes, if any anomalies are detected
           shut the well in following the hard procedure, then continue the running in the hole
            with stripping. Once on bottom, circulate a bottom-up volume and evaluate the
             situation.

  13.2 Gas Cap Drilling Policies

              Stay Focused on well control, while drilling through gas cap.

    Drilling in hydrocarbon-producing layers, especially Gas Reservoirs / Gas caps, is a high-risk
    activity and should be conducted with extreme cautions.

1. The approximate gas travel speed is 1100 ft (335 m) per second.

2. The difference in specific gravity of gas and drilling fluid is such that if after drilling 250 ft (80
   m) of the gas cap, a complete loss happens, mud level in the well will decrease to the point
   where the gas on at top of the gas cap would enter the well.

    Most of the well blowout cases, were due to the well and drilling string not being
             full. It is vital to keep the annulus and drill string full, all the times.

A) Gas Cap Drilling Regulations:
1. A sufficient amount of LCM (Loss Circulation Material) should always be available on rig site
   and used in the mud system in case of need, and the returned drilling fluid should not be
    diverted onto shale shakers. LCM concentration should not cause a disturbance in the
    circulation system. In Asmari formation, about 15-30 ppb LCM is typically used. LCM material
   should be used in water-based bentonite mud or high-viscosity CMC mud. Better to Avoid
   using LCM in freshwater.

2.  In case of happening complete loss, pick up the bit off bottom, so that tool joint is kept above
    the rotary table. Drill pipe connections should not be in front of BOPs.

             I.    During pull-out of the drill string, one pump should circulate mud/water through the
                  drill pipe, while another one should pump the mud in the annulus.

            II.    During pull out of the drill string, in case mud level in the annulus and the drill pipe
                is constantly decreasing, close pipe rams. And pump mud through the kill line.

2. Pump hi-viscosity pill with LCM, which has been prepared and always ready in pill tank during
     drilling of the gas cap, through the drill pipes. Trip tank always Should be fully in service.

3.   If mud loss is more than 50 BPH, do NOT pull the drill string completely out of the well in any
   circumstances. Unless in exceptions when the instruction to do so comes from Drilling
   headquarters. In this case, mud reserves equal to two times the loss amount should be ready
    in reserve tanks.

4.  Before POOH, circulate bottoms up and check in and out mud weight during circulation to
   ensure that a uniform mud with appropriate weight is inside the drill string and the annulus.

5.  During POOH, circulate mud through the wellhead to ensure the annulus is  full. While
   POOH/RIH mud tank volumes and return volume onto shale shakers should be constantly
   measured and loss rate constantly reported. Trip sheets should be carefully  filled and
   monitored by drilling supervisor and tool pusher.

                                                                                      Page 142 of 151

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                    Workover Program For
                       Well: BH-144, W.O.2, S.T.2
                        BiBi Hakimeh Oil Field

   Page 143 of 151             Doc. No: BH-15-SM-110-DR-DM-1789-D02                 Rev:02

6.  Before RIH a new drilling bit, the drilling supervisor should personally check the float valve to
   ensure it is functional and assemble it inside the drilling string. Sufficient spare parts should
   be available for the maintenance of the float valve.

7. Two 4 ½” safety valves with IF connections should be available on the rig floor. One of these
   valves should be assembled below Kelly during drilling operations. The second one should
   be available as standby for necessary cases.

8. Mud tanks should be full of drilling fluid and water tanks should be filled up with water. In case
    of water shortage and severe losses, Drilling superintendent should be informed.

9.  Rotating head should be used for drilling of gas cap. Regular pressure and function test of
    Rotating head should be performed according to NISOC well control Policy.

10. While RIH with a new drilling bit, it is advised that slip and cut or any other maintenance is
   conducted while the bit is at the shoe.

11. Running  deviation survey equipment should be done  after confirmation from  Drilling
   headquarters.

12. For casing running instruction, please refer to the related procedures.

13. If  it is allowed by drilling headquarters, based on the predicted mud loss rate,  it may be
    required that sufficient cement volume and pump truck be available/standby at the rig site.

14. Record the stand-pipe pressure with half the normal strokes per minute, at the beginning of
   each shift and when mud weight is changed. Perform SCR checks as per Policy.

15. Control Drilling Rate when severe losses are predicted.

16. Flow Check all Connections while drilling through Gas Cap.

B) BOP Equipment and BOP Drills:
1. BOP drills should be done periodically, and all personnel should be trained to activate the
  BOP equipment in emergencies.

2. A baker-type flapper float valve should be assembled above the drilling bit.

3.   if necessary, Open bit nozzles should be used for gas cap drilling so that it is possible to pump
    hi-vis pill and cement.

4.  Rig personnel should be trained on procedures for dropping drill collars inside well, in case of
   a blowout.

5.  Perform a condition  trip after running electrical logging equipment and before running
    casing/liner.

6.  In case a gas cut mud is observed, record and report it. In case of Gas cut mud return,  it
   should be considered as first option not to have enough and stable overbalance.

7.  For running liner, pipe rams for the liner size and drill pipe size should be installed on the BOP
    stack.
