13.3 H2S Monitoring and Prevention Measure

 Reservoir sections of BiBi Hakimeh oil field contain harmful H2S in oil and gas phases.

  The amount of H2S of Reservoir fluids has been reported to be equal to 0.01 % Mole (100
PPM) in Gas Cap of Asmari and 0.18 % Mole (1800 PPM) in oil phase and 0.34 % Mole (3400
PPM) in Associated gas of Asmari Reservoir, respectively. These values are subjected to
future samplings and well testing.

H2S Services would be used before entering Asmari Formation.

     General Requirements of H2S Service

  Contractor shall provide the H2S monitoring and control services to protect against high
concentrations of H2S gas expected through the drilling operation of Asmari Formation. Some
of main responsibilities are:
• To ensure all H2S Safety equipment is in good condition.
• To supply, maintain, inspect, calibrate and repair all H2S equipment to the highest standard
  and have this equipment ready to use all the time.
• To supply gas detectors & required accessories to automate the gas detection program with
   capability of continually evaluate the condition, collect information and provide automate &
  manual alerts regarding the status of the available toxic gases.
• To review Company’s H2S evacuation procedure and to advise where change/modifications
   are required.
• To monitor levels of H2S and other hazardous gases, inform Company, and take any
  necessary precautions to safeguard  life, the well and the Equipment from impending
   danger.
• Customize safety training program.
•  Maintain a minimum inventory of parts and accessories to facilitate ease and quality
   provision of service.
• To prepare and submit detailed technical and quality reports on timely basis.
• To keep available at rig site on 24 hours/day.
•  Contractors are obligated to issue their plan and coordination procedure 2 weeks prior to
   start the operation.
• The supply different types (fixed multi-sensor, portable, electronic and non-electronic,
  sampling type) of H2S gas detection equipment to be installed, tested and commissioned
  on rig that will be operative while drilling, complete with dual level (10-20ppm) visual and
   audible alarms at different points of the rig site, to allow for early warning of all personnel
  on the rig for H2S gas occurrence in the atmosphere, hence ease of following emergency
   plans to safety briefing areas. All equipment and service personnel shall be on location
   before drilling the hole section which contain H2S or other poisonous gases as called be
  Company.
•  Supply of a comprehensive cascade breathing air loop system, complete with breathing air
   supply compressors, storage cylinders, and work/escape and rescue breathing apparatus.

                                                                                   Page 144 of 151

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                   Workover Program For
                     Well: BH-144, W.O.2, S.T.2
                      BiBi Hakimeh Oil Field

 Page 145 of 151             Doc. No: BH-15-SM-110-DR-DM-1789-D02                 Rev:02

   This system shall provide at time of gas detection and alarm for essential personnel, who
    will be responsible to control the well during emergencies, also an extended period of time
  under fresh breathing air, to allow for non-essential personnel to escape to safe briefing
   areas, and for rescue teams to rescue any injured persons during H2S emergencies.
•  Safety supervision  shall be performed by  qualified personnel  at  rig  site,  to  install,
  commission and maintains  in good condition the H2S safety equipment, to train  rig
   personnel, Company and Third-Party personnel on H2S emergency procedures and use of
   the H2S safety equipment and to assist in carrying out the H2S Safety Plan.

      Monitoring H2S
• Somewhere on the well site is apt to accumulate H2S, such as mud pit, shale shaker, and
   drilling platform, should be installed one set of. Make sure H2S monitor and alarm system
  can be used.
• Alarm threshold setting for H2S warning indicator: the first warning level is 10mg/m3, unable
  warning sound, just show present value of H2S content to operators. The second warning
   level is 20mg/m3, when the H2S content in air exceed 20mg/m3, H2S warning indicator can
   give an alarm automatically, and warning sound should be heard by everyone work in well
   site. The audible alarm should be installed on Kelly board.
•  Equip enough gas masks on drilling platform, shaker screen (total amount should large than
   24), and equip 12 sets of 30min positive pressure Air Aspirators，2 sets of portable H2S
   monitor and appropriative H2S warning indicator. Arrange a person specially assigned for
   taking charge all the protective apparatus. This man must do periodical check to keep this
  equipment in a good condition.
• H2S monitor should be done weekly check and compulsory examination.
•  After drilling into the pay zone, strengthen the detection of H2S content in mud.
•  Contractor shall implement H2S service system by himself or 3rd party for controlling the
       risk of H2S leakage.

     Measures for H2S prevention
• Windsocks shall be set on the drill floor and in the monsoon direction or/and prevailing wind
   direction of the field, off well site. In emergency, employees must follow the evacuation
   procedure.
•  Explosion-proof blowers shall be installed around the rig, shale shaker, and drilling fluid
   tanks to blow away toxic and flammable gas at the well site.
• The rig crew in the H2S area shall be furnished with H2S monitors and adequate protective
  apparatus in accordance with IPS. Everybody shall be able to use, maintain, and check the
   apparatus.
• The technicians of rig crew are responsible for H2S proof training and the tool pusher is in
  charge of the supervision and examination.
• The pH value of drilling fluid in the H2S area shall be kept over 9.5. Pay attention to
  measurement of H2S concentration in drilling fluid. The H2S concentration shall be kept at
   less than 50mg/cm3 by using sulphur removal agents and degassers.

                                                                                   Page 145 of 151

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                   Workover Program For
                     Well: BH-144, W.O.2, S.T.2
                      BiBi Hakimeh Oil Field

 Page 146 of 151             Doc. No: BH-15-SM-110-DR-DM-1789-D02                 Rev:02

•  Personnel safety and protection shall be carried out according to IPS during operations in
   polluted areas where H2S content exceeds the critical concentration.
• When the rig crew makes decision to bleed and fire because well control cannot be
  performed due  to  well  site  conditions, operations  shall be performed according  to
  procedures in IPS.
• Once blowout occurs, the incident shall be reported to the higher-level department as soon
  as possible and firefighting trucks, ambulances, medical care personnel and technicians
   shall be on duty at the well site.
•  After the blowout is controlled, H2S concentration should be measured in the places where
  H2S is possible to gather.
• The personnel are not allowed to access until the H2S concentration is below the critical
   concentration.

     H2S Monitoring & Control

The H2S system equipment provided shall be designed for the safety-zone-area classification
where they are deployed. Equipment supplied by the service company for the purpose of the
work shall at all times be maintained in good conditions of the performance of the services.
Company’s Minimum Requirements for H2S Equipment are provided in below table:

            Company’s Minimum Requirements for H2S Equipment
